### X.W11.api

SERVED MODEL: claude-opus-5-5 · W11.md ADDENDUM 2026-10-06 limb (a) (`:504-505`) · COHESION §0eh · 2026-10-18.

**Crash-recovery.** ⟨`git status --porcelain -- <the 7 writable paths> docs/tranches/X/execution/A/X-W11.md`⟩ → empty. **Inherited paths: none.**

**Acts, in order.**

1. **Falsifier first** — `e2e/smoke/flows/palette-api-failure.spec.ts` (create). Three routes on `**/palettes?**`: `route.abort("failed")` (network/TLS), a `503`, and a malformed `200 text/html`. Each asserts: the Browse plate (`role=alert` in the main pane) says *"Couldn't reach the palette service."* and the pane does not say *"No palettes published yet."*; exactly ONE toast in glass's Toaster viewport (`region "Notifications"` → `listitem`) titled *"Couldn't reach the palette service"*, naming the failure (`/network|TLS/` · `/503/` · `/malformed/`) with a `Retry` button; after a burst of 2+ more failures (plate Retry), still exactly one toast; then the route turns healthy, and the toast's Retry brings back the wall (`article "Palette: After The Outage"`).
2. **RED** — ⟨`guarded --timeout 20 -- npx playwright test e2e/smoke/flows/palette-api-failure.spec.ts --project=smoke --workers=1`⟩ → `3 failed` · exit 1. The plate read: `"Couldn't load palettes.The palette service is not answering. Retry"` (abort) · `"Couldn't load palettes.Service Unavailable Retry"` (503) · `"Couldn't load palettes.Unexpected token '<', "<!doctype "... is not valid JSON Retry"` (malformed). No toast and no Toaster existed anywhere in the tree.
3. **Cure at the transport root** (all inside the writable set):
   - `api-problem.ts` — `ApiProblem` gains `kind` (`unreachable | server | malformed | client`), `isServiceFailure`, `summary`, plus `ApiProblem.unreachable()` and `ApiProblem.malformed()` (URN types `PROBLEM_UNREACHABLE` and `PROBLEM_MALFORMED`). `toApiProblem(e)` classifies `ApiUnavailableError` (the latch's network/TLS rejection) as `unreachable`, a 5xx `ApiProblem` as `server`, and a `SyntaxError` (unparseable body) as `malformed`. Anything else (a `DevMisconfigError`, a programming error) returns `null` and is rethrown as itself, so nothing is caught silently.
   - `palettes.ts` — every request goes through `palettesRequest`. A service failure goes to `reportServiceProblem`, and the caller receives the typed problem. A 4xx is the service answering, so its surface keeps the verdict and no toast is raised. A success clears the problem. `GET /palettes` checks its body shape (`isPalettePage`): a 200 that is not the list counts as `malformed`, never as an empty wall.
   - `useApiClient.ts` — `reportServiceProblem` raises glass's own `toast()` + `ToastAction` (consumed from `@mkbabb/glass-ui/toast`; nothing copied). It is one toast per burst: a report inside a standing toast's 10 s window `update`s that toast instead of stacking another. The toast's Retry closes it, calls `admitRecoveryProbe()` and re-runs the reloads registered through `onServiceRetry`. `serviceProblem` and `onServiceRetry` join the `ApiClient` DI seam.
   - `App.vue` — glass's `<Toaster />` mounted once.
   - `BrowsePane.vue` — the error plate's headline reads `serviceProblem`: *"Couldn't reach the palette service."* for a service failure, and *"Couldn't load palettes."* for a 4xx refusal. The wall registers `pm.retryRemotePalettes` for the toast's Retry.
4. **First GREEN attempt: RED on recovery.** After the healthy switch, the wall stayed **blank** after any Retry: no plate, no skeleton, no cards. The root cause was measured: `dc94d7745` (X-DS value pass 8) had put a leading `<template>` comment back into `EmptyState.vue`. In dev that makes the component multi-root, so `<Transition mode="out-in">` never finishes its leave. This is exactly the defect X.W7.g's ES-2 note (`EmptyState.vue` script) records. The two comments between `v-if` branches are folded into their branch by the dev compiler, so those branches were fragments too. Cure: each note moved inside its branch's element or into the script note. Each branch now renders one element.
5. **GREEN.** The spec's two burst presses now retry until each lands on the settled plate (`expect(...).toPass()`, with the failure count asserted inside). No assertion was weakened.

**Commit.** `6932b7541` — `fix(X·W11.api): palette-service failures surface — one glass toast with Retry, and a Browse plate that says the service couldn't be reached (§0eh limb (a))`. One commit holds the cure and the falsifier. ⟨`git show --stat --format= 6932b7541`⟩ → `8 files changed, 400 insertions(+), 27 deletions(-)`.

**Adjacent edits (§0bt).**
- `e2e/smoke/crash-battery.spec.ts:102` — the R14 oracle asserted the copy this unit changed. `/Couldn't load palettes/` became `/Couldn't reach the palette service\./`, because its route aborts and so is a service failure.
- `e2e/smoke/crash-battery.spec.ts:107` — `page.getByRole("button", { name: "Retry" })` is now `mainPane(page).getByRole(...)`, since the toast carries a second Retry and the unscoped locator would hit strict mode.
- No assertion was removed or weakened.

**Gates, BEFORE → AFTER.**

| gate | before | after |
|---|---|---|
| §0eh (a) e2e (abort · 503 · malformed) | ⟨playwright, smoke, workers=1, guarded⟩ `3 failed` · exit 1 | run 1 `3 passed (29.0s)` · exit 0; run 2 `3 passed (25.8s)` · exit 0 |
| lint | `lint exit 0` (open baseline) | ⟨`guarded --light -- npm run lint`⟩ exit 0 |
| vue-tsc demo | exit 0 (open baseline) | ⟨`guarded --light -- npx vue-tsc -p tsconfig.demo.json --noEmit`⟩ exit 0 |

**Regression reads (not gates; bounded).**
- ⟨`guarded --timeout 30 -- npx playwright test e2e/smoke/crash-battery.spec.ts e2e/smoke/oracles/o9-shadow-palette.spec.ts e2e/smoke/oracles/w7-mutation-visibility.spec.ts e2e/smoke/views/ e2e/smoke/flows/ --project=smoke --workers=1`⟩ → `66 passed (3.3m)` · exit 0. This includes R14 "Retry on a dead Browse wall".
- ⟨`guarded --light -- npx vitest run demo/test/palettes test/status-lamp.test.ts --maxWorkers=2`⟩ → `Test Files 21 passed (21)` · `Tests 163 passed (163)`.

**Residuals (named, owned).**
- R-api-1: `e2e/visual` browse goldens. The newest `at-rest-browse-*` goldens already show pre-existing copy (*"The commons is unreachable."*, not the tree's *"Couldn't load palettes."*), so they were stale before this unit. Any unrouted browse capture now also shows the toast and the new headline. Owner: `.d` (G7 browser cell) or the next visual re-baseline unit. Not measured here.
- R-api-2: `useBrowsePalettes.ts:100`'s `e instanceof ApiUnavailableError` branch no longer fires for `listPalettes`. It now receives the typed `ApiProblem` and words the detail from `problem.message` (*"No response — the network or the service's TLS certificate failed."*). The file is outside this unit's set. Behaviour is correct, but the branch is dead for this caller.
- R-api-3: when no surface has registered a reload (e.g. a mutation failing outside Browse), the toast's Retry admits the latch's recovery probe and closes the toast. The person re-issues the act.

### X.W11.smk

SERVED MODEL: claude-opus-5-5 · 2026-10-18 · sections executed: W11.md ADDENDUM 2026-10-06 limb (b) (`:506`); COHESION §0eh (b).

**Crash-recovery.** ⟨`git status --porcelain -- .github/workflows/deploy-pages.yml .github/workflows/api-tls-smoke.yml scripts/ci/api-tls-smoke.mjs`⟩ → empty. **Inherited paths: none.**

**Bounds read from the addendum's words (flagged, per the unit plan).** The target is `https://api.color.babb.dev/palettes?limit=1`. The assertions are 200 + JSON, strict TLS, and `notAfter` more than 14 days out. The check fails the deploy and also runs weekly on a schedule. The script's defaults are exactly these values. `--url` and `--min-days` exist only so the probe can be falsified, and CI runs the bare form.

**Acts, in order.**
1. Created `scripts/ci/api-tls-smoke.mjs` (Node builtins only). It makes one `https.get` with Node's default chain and hostname verification, and reads `res.socket.getPeerCertificate().valid_to` while the socket is still open. It fails, naming the cause, on any of these: a request or TLS error, `socket.authorized` false, status ≠ 200, a media type other than `application/json`, a body that `JSON.parse` rejects, or `notAfter ≤ now + 14 d`. It refuses to run when `NODE_TLS_REJECT_UNAUTHORIZED=0` is set. Nothing in it disables verification.
2. Added a final step to `.github/workflows/deploy-pages.yml`, after the deploy and O-25: `node scripts/ci/api-tls-smoke.mjs` (working dir `value.js`). A non-zero exit fails the deploy job.
3. Created `.github/workflows/api-tls-smoke.yml`. It runs on `schedule: "17 13 * * 1"` (weekly) plus `workflow_dispatch`, with `contents: read`, checkout, Node 24, and then the same command. There is no install step.

**Gate §0eh (b): BEFORE → AFTER.** Open baseline: ⟨`git grep -lE "notAfter|palettes\?limit=1" -- .github scripts e2e`⟩ → 0 files (RED). After (all runs via `guarded --light`):
- RED, non-JSON target: ⟨`node scripts/ci/api-tls-smoke.mjs --url https://color.babb.dev/`⟩ → `"failures":["content-type: expected application/json, got \"text/html; charset=utf-8\"","body: not JSON (Unexpected token '<', …)"]` · exit 1
- RED, threshold the live cert fails: ⟨`… --min-days 365`⟩ → `"failures":["cert: notAfter 2027-01-04T15:19:06.000Z is not more than 365 d out (77.8 d left)"]` · exit 1
- RED, strict TLS: ⟨`… --url https://expired.badssl.com/`⟩ → `request: CERT_HAS_EXPIRED certificate has expired` · exit 1. ⟨`… --url https://self-signed.badssl.com/`⟩ → `request: DEPTH_ZERO_SELF_SIGNED_CERT …` · exit 1
- RED, override refused: ⟨`NODE_TLS_REJECT_UNAUTHORIZED=0 node scripts/ci/api-tls-smoke.mjs`⟩ → `api-tls-smoke: NODE_TLS_REJECT_UNAUTHORIZED=0 is set — this smoke runs over strict TLS only` · exit 1
- GREEN ×2, live: ⟨`node scripts/ci/api-tls-smoke.mjs`⟩ run 1 and run 2 each → `{"ok":true,"status":200,"contentType":"application/json","subject":"api.color.babb.dev","issuer":"Let's Encrypt","notAfter":"2027-01-04T15:19:06.000Z","daysLeft":77.8,"minDays":14,"failures":[]}` · exit 0 (both runs taken after prettier's reformat, at the committed bytes)
- Cross-check: ⟨`openssl s_client -connect api.color.babb.dev:443 … | openssl x509 -noout -enddate`⟩ → `notAfter=Jan  4 15:19:06 2027 GMT`. This equals the socket's reading.

**Lint and format.** ⟨`actionlint .github/workflows/deploy-pages.yml .github/workflows/api-tls-smoke.yml`⟩ → exit 0 · ⟨`npx prettier --check scripts/ci/api-tls-smoke.mjs .github/workflows/*.yml`⟩ → `All matched files use Prettier code style!` · ⟨`npx eslint --max-warnings=0 scripts/ci/api-tls-smoke.mjs`⟩ → exit 0 · `git diff --check` → clean.

**Commit.** `aad381cbb` `ci(X·W11.smk): palette API smoke …`: one commit with three paths (+213 lines).

**Adjacent edits.** None.

**Residuals.**

- R-smk-2: the weekly workflow becomes active once this file reaches the default branch (`master`), at `.c`'s merge. GitHub runs `schedule` triggers from the default branch only.

**Acts, in order** (heavy runs via `~/.claude/guard/guarded`; scratch under the session scratchpad, never committed):
1. **Anchors at true bytes.** ⟨`grep -n "encodedToHsl\|finishChannels\|non_finite" src/color/*.ts`⟩ → `anchors.ts:167` `encodedToHsl` (divided `delta / (1 - |2L - 1|)`), `operations.ts:37` `finishChannels`, `operations.ts:52-55` the two non-finite checks before `finishChannels`. No drift from the fold's mechanism.
2. **Oracle FIRST, born RED.** Wrote `test/hsl-achromatic.test.ts` (6 cases: white; black; 256-grey sweep; `rgb(255,255,254)`; the 4,096-point 16-step lattice `k·17`; near-white `{250..255}³` + near-black `{0..5}³` = 216 + 216 edge cells). ⟨`guarded --light -- npx vitest run test/hsl-achromatic.test.ts`⟩ at `6ba17ea08` → **5 failed | 1 passed**: white `expected false to be true` (not ok); greys `[255]`; `rgb(255,255,254)` s = `1.000000000000085`; lattice **305** cells out of domain (e.g. `rgb(0,0,17) -> s=1.0000000000000004`); edge cells **27**. (Black passed.)
3. **Cure, at the model (CSS Color 4 §7.1 `rgbToHsl`).** `encodedToHsl`: `if (max === min || L === 0 || L === 1) return [0, 0, L]` before any division; else `s = (max − L) / min(L, 1 − L)`; hue from `encodedToHsv`; the powerless `"none"` is marked by `finishChannels` (`isPowerless("hsl")` reads `s·(1−|2l−1|) === 0`). Re-run → white, black, greys GREEN; **3 still RED**: `rgb(255,255,254)` s = `1.0000000000001132`, lattice **271**, edge **26** — the residual is not the formula.
4. **Residual rooted, not clamped.** Probe ⟨`guarded --light -- npx vite-node <scratch>/rt.ts`⟩: `rgb(255,255,255)→hwb` = `[336, 0.9999999999999997, -2.22e-16]` (encoded max = 1.0000000000000002 after `encodedSrgbToXyz → xyzToEncodedSrgb`); `rgb(1,1,1)→hsl` hue `340`, s `3.3e-16`. Root: `convertColor` sent rgb↔hsl/hsv/hwb through the XYZ matrix pair, so an in-gamut 8-bit input left the round trip perturbed. Cure: `anchors.ts` `SRGB_ENCODED_ANCHORS` (`toEncoded`/`fromEncoded` for rgb·hsl·hsv·hwb; `CONVERSION_ANCHORS`' four entries are now composed from it, `encodedFamilyAnchor`) and `operations.ts` `convertColor` routes a member→member conversion through encoded sRGB directly (`viaEncodedSrgb`), every other pair through XYZ (`viaXyz`); both keep the finite check before `finishChannels`. This is the conversion ORDER, which lives in `operations.ts` (the grant's condition). No clamp of `s` anywhere (⟨`git show 83964c120 -- src | grep -E '^\+' | grep -cE 'Math\.(min|max)\(1|clamp'`⟩ → 0; the one added `Math.min` is the spec's `min(L, 1 − L)` denominator).

## CI-GREEN (§0gc)

### 2026-10-18 · seat oracle-slate — job `oracle slate` (`node scripts/ci/oracle-slate.mjs`)

**Symptom (CI).** `Error: spawnSync git ENOBUFS` at `trackedFiles` (`oracle-slate.mjs:93`, `git ls-files -z`). Oldest sampled red with the same error: run 37870074859 (2026-10-09); newest 39117135689 (2026-10-18). From then on the crash hid every slate verdict.

**Root cause (measured).** The `git()` helper called `execFileSync` with no `maxBuffer`, so it got Node's default of 1 MiB (1,048,576 B). The size of `git ls-files -z` tracks the size of the tree, and the tree only grows:

| commit | date | `git ls-tree -r -z --name-only <c> \| wc -c` |
|---|---|---|
| ec6541584 (slate lands) | 2026-09-18 | 607,859 |
| df7687de8 | 2026-09-24 | 936,529 |
| a70f0b344 | 2026-09-25 | 1,035,767 |
| 04fb1b0f6 | 2026-10-08 | 1,280,629 (> 1 MiB) |
| HEAD bb20b5ca6 | 2026-10-18 | 2,041,222 B · 27,861 paths |

Breakdown at HEAD: 9,320 under `docs/tranches/X/evidence`, 4,432 `X/keyframes`, 3,173 `V/megatranche`, 2,676 `X/waves`. By extension: 8,154 png · 4,285 json · 3,745 md · 3,263 mjs · 3,186 txt. All of it is deliberately committed, dated evidence (E-3). It is not junk, and this seat does not escalate it as junk. The defect was the fixed ceiling.

**Cure (root).** `scripts/ci/oracle-slate.mjs`: `git()` now passes `maxBuffer: Infinity`, so there is no fixed ceiling. A larger constant would only defer the same bug as the tree grows. `trackedFiles()` is also memoized, because it was listed five times per run and the tracked set cannot change mid-run. The slate's own `npx playwright --list` and `find` calls already carry 64 MiB, so they were not touched.

**Slate run 1 (after the ENOBUFS cure).** `~/.claude/guard/guarded --light --timeout 15 -- node scripts/ci/oracle-slate.mjs`, EXIT 1:
- A/B/C clean: 8 projects declared, all 8 invoked; 113 tracked specs, 113 collected.
- E: 537 literal references checked, 124 UNCHECKABLE (reported). F: 22 globs read.
- `SLATE RED — 5 finding(s)`:
  - D ×3: o21, o22, o24.
  - E ×2 · DEAD ATTRIBUTE:
    - `e2e/smoke/w12-dock.spec.ts binds [data-glass-owned]`
    - `e2e/smoke/w12u-p-drag.spec.ts binds [data-dock-seat]`

**E cure.** Neither attribute exists in any product byte or in `node_modules/@mkbabb/glass-ui/dist` (grep: 0 hits; the only `dock-seat` there is the CSS custom property `--dock-seat`). Both were dead OR-branches of probe selectors, so neither could ever match. Removing them is behavior-identical:
- `w12-dock.spec.ts:374`: dropped `|| e.closest("[data-glass-owned]") === e` from `glassy()`.
- `w12u-p-drag.spec.ts:167`: dropped `[data-dock-seat], ` from the seat `closest()`.

Both files were clean in `git status --porcelain` before the edit. No assertion changed.

**Slate run 2.** Same command, EXIT 1, `SLATE RED — 3 finding(s)`, D only:
- `o21`: `oracles/o21-gradient-rail` (declares) vs `oracles/o21-space-catalog-truth` (SILENT)
- `o22`: `oracles/o22-specimen-legibility` (SILENT) vs `oracles/o22-status-lamp` (declares)
- `o24`: `oracles/o24-specimen-dot-identity` (SILENT) vs `perf/o24-lcp-identity` (declares)

**Escalation ESC-W11-ORACLE-ORDINAL-R47.** These are the R47 / NG-14 collisions that X-W1-FOLD §R47 (`:792-807`) predicted: W6-created oracles minted on ordinals that shipped oracles already held. They are genuinely different oracles, so making them "declare" each other's token would be a mask. The honest cure is to renumber the W6-created specs, which renames files cited bare in 61 to 152 tracked records each (`git grep -l`). That is an identity and corpus ruling owned by X-W6 / X-W1, outside this seat's grant, so it is returned and not acted on. The slate stays RED on D until that ruling lands.

### producer-plate-mass — 2026-10-18 (seat: claude-opus-5-5; jobs `producer / Node 22`, `producer / Node 24`)

**Root cause (measured, two layers).** `demo/test/palettes/plate-mass.test.ts` mounts `demo/shared/ui/PaletteCardSkeleton.vue` with a dynamic `import("/@fs…")` after the harness page has loaded. That SFC imports the `@mkbabb/glass-ui` ROOT barrel (`PaletteCardSkeleton.vue:113`), which the harness entry graph (`n-fixtures/harness/main.ts`, glass subpaths only) never reaches. (1) Vite's dep scanner crawls only the root's `index.html`, so on a cold cache the optimizer discovers `@mkbabb/glass-ui` at runtime, re-bundles, and forces a full page reload in the middle of the 390 px `page.evaluate`. That reload produces "Execution context was destroyed", and the null box follows from it. The 1440 px describe then passes because the cache is warm. (2) The shared `node_modules/.vite-harness` cache: Vite's dep hash omits `optimizeDeps.entries`. When `palette-card-layout.test.ts` (the bare harness) writes the cache first, as it does in the full `npm test` ordering, the plate seat logs "Hash is consistent. Skipping", skips its own scan, and hits the same runtime discovery. The hypothesis in the brief was correct for layer (1). Layer (2) is why a cure for (1) alone stayed red under the whole `npm test`.

**Cure (harness, no assertion/budget touched).** `demo/test/palettes/n-fixtures/browser.ts`: `openHarness(viewport, { mounts })`. A suite declares the modules it mounts after load, and these are passed as `optimizeDeps.entries: ["index.html", ...mounts]` so the pre-scan covers their imports before the first navigation. `cacheDir` is keyed by the entry set: `.vite-harness` when there are no mounts (unchanged for the two bare suites), `.vite-harness-<sha256(repo-relative mounts)[:12]>` otherwise. `plate-mass.test.ts` declares its `PLATES` files.

**Commands and measured output** (cold = `rm -rf node_modules/.vite-harness* node_modules/.vite/vitest` first; every run through `~/.claude/guard/guarded`, vitest `--maxWorkers=2`):
- PRE-CURE cold reproduction: `DEBUG=vite:deps guarded --timeout 10 -- npx vitest run --maxWorkers=2 demo/test/palettes/plate-mass.test.ts` → exit 1; `vite:deps new dependencies found: @mkbabb/glass-ui`; `× plate at 390 px > G20 … page.evaluate: Execution context was destroyed`, `× … N-17`; 1440 ✓✓; `Tests 2 failed | 2 passed (4)`. This matches CI 39188667296 / 39117135689 / 39034378132.
- Layer-(1) cure only, cold plate-mass ×3 → exit 0 ×3, `new dependencies found` 0 ×3, `Tests 4 passed (4)`. Then cold `guarded --timeout 40 -- npm test -- --maxWorkers=2` → exit 1, `Tests 2 failed | 1251 passed (1253)` (plate 390 G20/N-17; `palette-card-layout` ran first). The same palettes-dir run with debug showed `vite:deps (client) Hash is consistent. Skipping.` on the plate seat. That identified layer (2).
- Full cure, adversarial order (cold; bare suite writes the cache first): `vitest run … palette-card-layout.test.ts` → `Tests 6 passed (6)`; then `DEBUG=vite:deps … plate-mass.test.ts` → exit 0, new-deps 0, `Tests 4 passed (4)`; dirs `node_modules/.vite-harness` + `node_modules/.vite-harness-e960dedb20bc`.
- Full cure, **3 consecutive COLD** plate-mass runs → `cold1 exit 0 newdeps=0 Tests 4 passed (4)` · `cold2 exit 0 newdeps=0 Tests 4 passed (4)` · `cold3 exit 0 newdeps=0 Tests 4 passed (4)`. G20 390: plates 42 / 124.109375 at both k=1 and k=16; N-17 390: loading 124.109375 vs settled 124.125.
- Full cure, **cold whole `npm test`**: `guarded --timeout 40 -- npm test -- --maxWorkers=2` → exit 0, `Test Files 97 passed (97)`, `Tests 1253 passed (1253)`; `✓ test/ink.test.ts (27 tests)`. The five §0gb.2 `test/producer-held.json` rows pass through the held helper (held-passing).
- `guarded --light -- npx vue-tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.test.json` → no errors.

**Verdict:** CURED locally at the root. CI confirmation rides the push of this commit (producer / Node 22 + Node 24 on `ci.yml`).

**Evidence defect cured — `48c2009c5` (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`).** The `lhci-report` upload logged `No files were found with the provided path: .lighthouseci/` on every run. `actions/upload-artifact@v4` defaults to `include-hidden-files: false`, and `.lighthouseci/` is a dot-directory. The commit adds `include-hidden-files: true`. No threshold, step or setting of the gate changed. First run carrying it: `39198034188` (`gh run download 39198034188 -n lhci-report`) → 3 LHR JSON + 3 HTML + `assertion-results.json`.

**CI LHRs at `48c2009c5`.** Host `HeadlessChrome/155.0.0.0` (branded Chrome, new headless), benchmarkIndex 1988–2494.

| run | FCP | LCP | TBT | CLS | TTI | mainthread `other` | scripting |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 5456 | 6763 | 145738 | 0 | 174983 | 168110 ms | 4912 ms |
| 2 | 5122 | 6140 | 144942 | 0 | 175763 | 170033 ms | 3168 ms |
| 3 | 5116 | 6122 | 144705 | 0 | 175580 | 169932 ms | 3227 ms |

- `runWarnings`: "The page loaded too slowly to finish within the time limit".
- `main-thread-tasks` (run 2): 522 tasks spanning 7–45071 ms observed, busy 8.5 / 10.0 / 10.0 / 9.9 / 5.0 s per 10 s bucket. So the main thread is **100 % busy for Lighthouse's whole 45 s window**.
- Tasks after 20 s: p10/p50/p90/p99 = 80 / 83 / 89 / 92 ms. That is a render loop of ~12 frames/s, each ~83 ms of non-script (`other`) work.
- Under `throttlingMethod: simulate` (cpuSlowdownMultiplier 4), each 83 ms task becomes ~330 ms, i.e. ~280 ms blocking. 12/s over 45 s ≈ 150 s, which is the observed 144–146 s.

**TBT root cause (measured on CI's exact browser).** Capability probe in an amd64 container: Google Chrome 155.0.8059.84, `--headless=new` (branded Chrome named as the witness because CI runs it; headless shell does not reproduce). `gpucap/cap.html` reads:
- `webgl2: true`
- unmasked renderer `ANGLE (Google, Vulkan 1.3.0 (SwiftShader Device (Subzero) (0x0000C0DE)), SwiftShader driver)`
- `failIfMajorPerformanceCaveat:true` → still a context, so a caveat-based gate would not fire here
- `navigator.gpu` present, `requestAdapter()` → `null`

Consequences:
- The atmosphere is already correct. `useAtmosphere` → `resolveRenderMode("auto")` → `"css"`, so the aurora never arms on this device.
- The hero bead (glass-ui `Blob`, WebGPU-first, no adapter → WebGL2 net) renders a live metaball frame through the CPU rasteriser every rAF. Chrome's software compositor then reads it back ("GPU stall due to ReadPixels" in the console).

Ablation, same browser, same build, LoAF + longtask observers, 15 s late window (`loaf.mjs`, `--no-sandbox`, 412×823 mobile):
- WITH the bead: **172 long tasks / 11,084 ms busy**, no script attribution (non-script work).
- HeroBlob chunk withheld (404): **0 / 0**.

The loop never parks: glass-ui `createCanvasLifecycle` keeps rescheduling while `shouldContinue()`. A real GPU-less or GPU-blocklisted user gets the same wedged main thread.

**The cure, built and measured. NOT LANDED (escalated, see below).** Patch: `docs/tranches/X/evidence/X-W11-lhci/heroblob-software-raster-tier.patch`. It adds a mount-time software-raster tier in `demo/picker/visual/HeroBlob.vue`:
- **Detect:** `isSoftwareWebGLRenderer()`, the producer's own probe, the same tier the aurora answers.
- **Park:** after `rendererStatus.phase === "ready"` plus two frames, `pause()`. This is the producer's own reduced-motion contract: paint, then park.
- **Repaint:** one synchronous frame per `heroRegister` change (`resume(); pause();`, `flush: "post"`).

Identity colour and form are kept; motion is gated on that device tier only. Typecheck: `vue-tsc -p tsconfig.demo.json` clean outside the worktree-only `api/` dependency errors.

Measured on Chrome 155 amd64:
- With the cure: **0 long tasks / 0 ms** in the 15 s late window (the run before 172 / 11,084).
- Recolour by keyboard (B channel ×12 PageUp), then a quiet 6 s:
  - cure: 0 long tasks; the bead's own pixels re-tint (1,952 of 9,115 bead px changed, mean B 251.8 → 250.5)
  - base: 48 long tasks; 1,921 bead px changed, mean B 252.1 → 251.1
- Shots: `~/Programming/value-js-lhci-seat-run/shots/{base,cure}-*.png`. The bead is drawn with the same body under the cure.

**Why it is not landed (ESC-W11-LHCI-SOFTWARE-RASTER-BEAD).** The e2e smoke projects launch Chromium with `--use-angle=swiftshader --enable-unsafe-swiftshader` (`playwright.config.ts:80`). Four owner-traced specs require the hero bead **live on that software rasteriser**:
- `e2e/smoke/w12-picker-blob.spec.ts:76-84` — "idle: the bead still moves after 5 s", traced to the owner's 2026-09-23 *"the blob is awful, not animated"*.
- `e2e/smoke/webgl-blob-idle.spec.ts` gate 1 "LIVE — over an idle window with no input the hero draws (> 0)". Its own text: "the live hero's software draw runs p50 58-158 ms there — a software-raster floor".
- `e2e/smoke/perf/idle-frame-budget.spec.ts`
- `e2e/smoke/oracles/o12-blob-seat.spec.ts` O-12·3

The cure parks exactly that device, so it would red e2e-smoke. Rewriting those assertions would loosen standing gates. On a software-raster device the two standing gates are mutually exclusive: G-4 TBT ≤ 300 on a GPU-less runner, and a live bead under SwiftShader. **Ruling needed (orchestrator / owner):**
- (A) The software-raster tier binds. Land the patch, and the four live-bead specs move to "live on a GPU renderer; paint-then-park on a software one" by owner act.
- (B) G-4 runs where the bead's GPU path is real (a GPU runner).
- (C) A producer-side (glass-ui) software path whose per-frame main-thread cost stays under 12.5 ms observed. Cadence caps cannot do it: at 1 fps, 45 frames × ~280 ms blocking ≈ 12.7 s TBT.

**Even with the loop parked, TBT and LCP stay red. Measured, two residuals.**

Lighthouse in an arm64 Linux container: `mcr.microsoft.com/playwright:v1.60.0-noble`, headless shell, `--cpus 4 --memory 12g --shm-size=2g`, `@lhci/cli@0.15.x autorun --config=value.js/lighthouserc.json`, 3 runs each, only `--collect.settings.chromeFlags=--no-sandbox` added (container root). ### e2e-visual — 2026-10-19 (seat: claude-opus-5-5; jobs `e2e-smoke`, `visual`, `e2e-safari`, `boot-smoke`)

**What gates the deploy.** `.github/workflows/deploy-pages.yml` triggers on `workflow_run` of `ci.yml` (`types: [completed]`) and its job runs only when `conclusion == 'success' && head_branch == 'master' && event == 'push'`. A run's conclusion is `success` only when every job passes. So all ten `ci.yml` jobs must be green, on a push to master. A green `tranche-u` run never deploys. Every `deploy-pages` run listed (`gh run list --workflow deploy-pages.yml -L 8`) reads `skipped`.

**Why no run completed.** `gh run list --branch tranche-u --workflow ci.yml -L 100`: only 39117135689 (`fd3865620`) and 39034378132 completed. Every later run was `cancelled` by `concurrency: cancel-in-progress`, because pushes landed about every 20 min and the browser jobs take 1.6–2.2 h. In 39117135689, `boot-smoke` and `e2e-safari` were cancelled at the GitHub 6 h job ceiling (09:55 → 15:56). Run 39204638324 (`dfc4a0827`, which carries this seat's commits) completed `boot-smoke`, `visual` and `e2e-smoke`; `e2e-safari` was still running at writing time.

#### boot-smoke — harness defect, CURED (`c1fb8958a`)
- **Symptom.** Run 39117135689 logged `[boot-smoke] dev: 8/8 seed cases passed` at 09:58:19. The next line is `##[error]The operation was canceled.` at 15:55:51. Cleanup then printed `Terminate orphan process: pid (3110)`.
- **Root cause.** The script spawned `npx vite …` with piped stdio and stopped it with `child.kill("SIGTERM")`. On Linux, `npx` dies on SIGTERM but leaves its vite grandchild running. Vite is re-parented to init and keeps the pipe write ends open, so node never exits. macOS hides the bug.
- **Reproduction.** Minimal reproduction in `node:24` with a fake bin: `npx exit null SIGTERM` · `STILL ALIVE after 5s — loop held` · orphan `28 1 node …/fakevite`. Real reproduction in `mcr.microsoft.com/playwright:v1.60.0-noble`: `timeout 180 node <HEAD boot-smoke> --mode=dev --seed=default` → `dev: 1/1 seed cases passed`, `HEAD dev rc=124`, and an orphan `39 1 node /w/node_modules/.bin/vite --force …` remains.
- **Cure** (`scripts/ci/boot-smoke.mjs`):
  - spawn `node_modules/vite/bin/vite.js` under `process.execPath`, `detached: true`;
  - `stop()` signals the process group (`process.kill(-pid)`), waits for `exit` with a cancellable 5 s grace, then SIGKILLs the group and destroys the pipes;
  - `chromium.launch` moves inside the `try`, so a failed launch still stops the server.
- **Verification.**
  - Same container, cured: `dev: 8/8` rc=0 in 16 s; `prod-preview: 8/8` rc=0; `ps` shows no processes left.
  - Darwin, `guarded --timeout 10`: `dev: 8/8` rc=0, `no vite left`.
  - **CI run 39204638324, job 117905058813: `boot-smoke` success.** `dev: 8/8` at 05:32:03, `prod-preview: 8/8` at 05:32:27, about 4 min for the job.
- No seed, assertion or mode was changed.

#### e2e-safari — platform defect, CURED at the runner (`f71767898`); 25 ruled rows remain
- **Symptom.** 39117135689: `Running 176 tests using 1 worker`, then `××T` across the board, then `worker-128 process did not exit within 300000ms after stop`, then the 6 h cancel. No report artifact (see the reporter cure below).
- **Reproduction in `mcr…playwright:v1.60.0-noble`** (`guarded --exclusive webkit`, `--workers=1 --retries=0`): **175 failed · 3 passed (23.0 m)**. Error classes: `Target page, context or browser has been closed` ×25+, `toBeVisible` ×51, `Page crashed` ×3, and `networkidle` 60 s timeouts.
- **Probes** (`wk-probe*.mjs`, one browser, contexts closed in `finally`):
  - Bare WebGL2 platform: available; an fbm draw at 1280×720 takes 4–5 ms after the first (236 ms).
  - App at the built origin: `lat=2ms` until t≈2.8 s, then `{"timeout":8000}`. Same result under `reducedMotion: reduce` (wedged at t≈9.7 s). Same on the dev origin (`page.evaluate` never returned; 120 s).
  - Arm `no-webgl` (getContext("webgl*") → null): 24/24 evaluates answer in 1–74 ms and the dock mounts.
  - Arm `gl-timed`: wedged from t≈11 s, 4× 10 s timeouts.
  - Cause: the main thread wedges inside the WebGL path of Linux WebKit (WPE/GTK over Mesa). That is not Safari's graphics stack.
- **Cure.** `e2e-safari` now runs on `macos-15`, the same platform law the `visual` job states.
- **Darwin verification** (`VJS_E2E_PORT=9471 … guarded --exclusive webkit --timeout 85 -- npx playwright test --project=smoke-safari --project=oracles-safari --workers=1 --retries=0`): **153 passed · 25 failed (13.3 m)**; smoke-safari 4/4 (`sustained-30s console-failures: 0`).
  - Note: the darwin run used the working tree, which carries X-DS's uncommitted `demo/palettes/admin/panels/Admin{Flagged,Tags,Users}Panel.vue`.
- **The 25 that remain are ESC-W10-h-1 rows, outside this seat:**
  - o18 ×7 (E1–E4)
  - o19 ×4 (E5)
  - o23 (E9)
  - o28 (E7)
  - o11 gate 4 and o4 ×2 (E15)
  - o15b and o27 ×2 (E16)
  - w7-destructive-seats ×6 (E17)
- Not yet confirmed on the CI runner. That reading needs the first `macos-15` e2e-safari run.

#### e2e-smoke — product and ruled rows remain; the report artifact is CURED (`80734737c`)

### 2026-10-19 · repair r1 · `deploy-age` (run 39229786770 @ 113b00bb1)

**Measured (job 117985253165).** `gh run view --job 117985253165 --log` → `newest successful deploy : 2026-07-07T02:19:20Z [gh run list --workflow deploy-pages.yml --status success]` · `newest recorded close : 2026-10-19T01:22:22-04:00 [git log -- docs/tranches (subject names a close)]` · `FAIL — production is older than the newest close by 104 day(s).` Local repro: `git log -1 --date=iso-strict --format='%h %cd %s' -E --grep='(close|CLOSE|CLOSED|FINAL)' 113b00bb1 -- docs/tranches` → `7c0fb2710 2026-10-19T01:22:22-04:00 docs(X-DS close r3): …`. Both inputs were measured and neither override was set. The reading is TRUE: `gh run list --workflow deploy-pages.yml --status success --limit 3 --json createdAt,headSha,event` → newest `2026-07-07T02:19:20Z`, `workflow_dispatch`, `80c58885…`. `origin/master` = `04d2d8089` (2026-09-18), and `git rev-list --count origin/master..HEAD` = 2843.

**Root cause: the gate self-locks. It is not self-curing.** Earlier receipts in this file (G6 rows above) said "`deploy-age` cures itself on the first real deploy." That is false. The measurement shows why:
1. `deploy-pages.yml` ships only when the **whole** `CI` workflow concludes `success` on a master push (`if: … workflow_run.conclusion == 'success' && head_branch == 'master' && event == 'push'`).
2. `deploy-age` is a job **inside** that same `CI` workflow.
3. The master push that would deploy carries the newest close itself. So on that push, `CLOSE_AT` > `DEPLOY_AT`, `deploy-age` reds, `CI` concludes `failure`, and the deploy is `skipped`.
Witness: the landing master push `35381701436` (2026-09-18, "Merge tranche-u into master — X-W1 · CC-033 · G-18") measured `newest successful deploy : 2026-07-07T02:19:20Z` · `newest recorded close : 2026-09-18T14:36:11-04:00` · `FAIL — … by 73 day(s).`, and **no** deploy-pages run succeeded after it (`gh run list --workflow deploy-pages.yml --limit 200` filtered to 2026-09-18T18..09-19T08 → `[]`; the five newest deploy-pages runs are all `skipped`).
Once production is older than any close, the only path to `deploy-age` green is a deploy. The only deploy path that does not need `deploy-age` green first is `workflow_dispatch`, which this law forbids because it bypasses the declared green-CI gate. So this deadlock holds on its own, independent of the other red jobs (oracle slate, lhci, e2e-smoke, visual, e2e-safari). Curing every one of them still leaves the master push red here.

A secondary defect (input precision, not the cause): the close grep matches any `docs/tranches` subject containing `close`, e.g. `X-DS close r3`, a sibling review pass, not a tranche close. Narrowing it would not change the verdict, because the 2026-09-18 landing close is itself 73 days past the deploy.

**Why nothing was landed.** The honest cure changes the deploy gate's topology: what `deploy-pages` waits on, and where `deploy-age` measures. Options for a ruling:
- **(A)** Move `deploy-age` out of `CI` into a post-deploy check: a step after the ship in `deploy-pages.yml`, plus a scheduled workflow that reds when production is older than the newest master close. Then the master push's `CI` (every other job) gates the deploy, and `deploy-age` measures whether that deploy happened.
- **(B)** Keep it in `CI` but compare against the close reachable from the **previous** deployed sha, so the push carrying the close can still ship.
Both redefine the declared green-CI deploy gate and DR-11's G-21 instrument. Any guard of the form `if: ref != master` would mask the gate, which is forbidden. So this is returned as an escalation (ESC-W11-DEPLOY-AGE-SELF-LOCK), not landed. No file except this receipt was changed. No override variable, threshold or workflow_dispatch was used.

**Verdict:** NOT CURED in r1. The reading is a true DR-11 red, and the gate as designed cannot clear without an owner/orchestrator ruling on (A) or (B), followed by a green-CI master push.

- Local `guarded --timeout 15 -- node scripts/ci/oracle-slate.mjs` at 623742b8e (origin has no newer commits) gives the same 3 D findings. A/B/C are clean (8/8 projects, 113/113 collected). The D notes for o10d and o18 still read correctly as one oracle over two files.
- Origin (`git log --diff-filter=A`): the three declaring specs are T-era (o24 5cdccb529 2026-07-10; o21 ee7b1dc50 and o22 e4ddd3278 2026-07-11). The three SILENT specs were all minted together at e0e204a9b on 2026-09-19 (X:CSS-1). Their headers name themselves `X.W6.f · X:CSS-1 gate f3 / f4 / f8` and carry no `O-21/22/24` token. These are exactly the collisions X-W1-FOLD §R47 (`:792-807`) predicted for W6's created oracles.

**Why there is no in-grant cure.** The predicate is right: these are two different oracles under one ordinal. A `scripts/ci` change that stops reporting them would loosen the R47 gate, and adding a foreign `O-2x` token to the SILENT specs would be a mask. Both are forbidden. The root cure is to renumber the three W6 specs, which is an identity and corpus ruling outside this seat's grant:
- Executable references: `git grep -l <name> -- ':!docs'` returns 0 for all three. The rename touches no ci.yml, playwright config or script, so the files would be the only executable change.
- Bare citations in tracked records: `o21-space-catalog-truth` 117, `o22-specimen-legibility` 90, `o24-specimen-dot-identity` 74. Those records are dated and append-only (E-3), so the rename needs an alias note in COHESION and cannot be done by rewriting them.
- The next ordinals are not clean: `git grep -lwi` gives o30 150, o31 159, o32 221 (O-30 7, O-31 18, O-32 29). Choosing new ordinals is therefore itself an identity ruling for X-W6 / X-W1, not something a seat can pick.

**Disposition.** Not cured. No files changed except this receipt. The escalation ESC-W11-ORACLE-ORDINAL-R47 stands, now with the measurements above. The ruling it needs: assign the three X:CSS-1 gates (f3 / f4 / f8) fresh, uncited ordinals (or a non-`oNN` prefix, which the slate's `/^(o\d+[a-z]*)-/` does not claim) and record the old-to-new alias in COHESION. Then `git mv` the three specs (they are clean in `git status --porcelain`) under X-W6's grant. The slate D section then goes green with no slate change.

**Commands and measured output.**
1. `gh run view --job 117985252506 --log` → 5906 lines; `Running 178 tests using 1 worker` 09:30:35Z; `72 failed · 11 flaky · 95 passed (2.5h)`. `gh run download 39229786770 -n e2e-safari-report` → HTML report (41 JSON + 157 error-context.md); per-attempt timeline extracted (334 attempts, CI `retries: 2`).
2. Error-class tags over every non-passed attempt (report JSON): `WebGL: context lost.` console errors 18 (in o17, o21-rail, o27); `PaneChunkError: pane chunk not settled after 20000 ms` 10 (the app's own chunk deadline; o21-rail, o27); `the region never settled` (o29 ×6); `toBeAttached` on the bead canvas (o12 ×3, dual-webgl:163); o2 `field absent … draws=0`; o26 `0.000 < 4/255`; o28 `no ground write precedes first paint (NaNms)`; o18 `bootAtDefaultSeed` polls on `--accent-live` / `--ink-ambient-l` (written by `{ immediate: true }` watchers in `useAtmosphereBoot.ts`), timed out at 8 s, which means the page's `page.evaluate` did not return.
3. **The CI renderer self-reports a degraded GPU.** `[o12-backing] renderer=REAL-GPU backing 93x93 css 180.2x180.2 dpr 1 → ratio 0.516` and `88x88 … 0.488`: the bead's governor shrank its backing store because frames were slow. The same leg on darwin hardware (step 5) gives `backing 180x180 … ratio 0.999`.
4. **Clustering.** Failures come by spec file, not at random. Tests without a live-WebGL dependency run at local speed on CI: o10 3–5 s, o14 4–9 s, t31 3–11 s, w7-mutation 3–6 s. Every spec that sets `test.use({ deviceScaleFactor: 2 })` (o19, o21-gradient-rail, o27), which means 4× the GPU pixels, fails in every test, and so does smoke-safari (iPhone 14, DPR 3), 4/4.
5. **Same specs, same tree, real Apple GPU.** `rm -rf node_modules/.vite-e2e-9563; VJS_E2E_PORT=9563 VJS_E2E_PERF_PORT=9564 guarded --exclusive webkit --timeout 45 -- npx playwright test --project=oracles-safari --project=smoke-safari --workers=1 --retries=0 o17 o29 o27 o12 o2 o11 o19 o21-space-catalog o26 e2e/smoke/safari` gave **32 passed · 7 failed (4.6 m)**, cold cache. The 7 are all ruled rows: o11 gate 4 (E15), o19 ×4 (E5), o27:71 and o27:201 (E16). o17 5/5, o29 6/6, o12 4/4, o2, o26 (`renderer=Apple GPU`), o21-space-catalog, and smoke-safari 4/4 (`sustained-30s` 33.3 s) all pass. These are the cells that fail on CI.
6. `rm -rf node_modules/.vite-e2e-9561; … guarded --exclusive webkit --timeout 30 -- npx playwright test --project=oracles-safari --workers=1 --retries=0 cold-nav-scene-enter o21-gradient-rail` (with `DEBUG=pw:webserver`) gave **12 passed (1.2 m)**: o21-rail 10/10 in 2.5–5.3 s each, against 30 s timeouts on CI. A side finding: cold vite logged `✨ new dependencies optimized: katex` mid-run, which is a runtime dep discovery and so a full reload. It reloaded harmlessly here. It is not the cause of this delta, and the cure would be `vite.config.ts` `optimizeDeps`, which is outside this seat's grant; noted for the vite owner.
7. Platform: GitHub's runner reference states that arm64 macOS runners do not support Metal Performance Shaders, a limit of Apple's Virtualization Framework. The October 2023 M1 larger-runner beta post said GPU acceleration was on by default. That conflicts with current docs and is not verified here.

**Root cause.** The macos-15 hosted runner is an Apple-Virtualization VM with no real GPU acceleration. On it, WebKit's WebGL path does three things: it loses contexts (`WebGL: context lost.`), it starves the governor (backing 0.49–0.52), and it stalls the web process. That stall misses the app's 20 s pane-chunk deadline and the 8 s/30 s test deadlines. This is the Linux-WPE wedge recorded in the prior receipt, in milder form. The prior "cure at the runner" (`f71767898`) was verified on the owner's Mac, which has a real Apple GPU, and not on the hosted VM. On the hosted VM it fails.

**What would mask it, and so was not done.** Withholding or disabling WebGL in the WebKit projects, forcing DPR 1, raising the 20 s / 30 s / 8 s deadlines, skipping or excluding the WebGL specs, or `continue-on-error`. Each of these removes the engine-specific WebGL coverage the project exists for.

**Disposition.** Not cured in-seat. No files changed except this receipt.
- **ESC-W11-SAFARI-HOSTED-GPU (new; owner act).** e2e-safari needs a runner with real GPU-accelerated WebKit. That means a self-hosted Apple-silicon runner, or a hosted size whose GPU is verified. Both are owner billing or infrastructure decisions.
- ESC-W11-SAFARI-RULED-ROWS (25 darwin cells) stands unchanged.
- The `CDP session is only available in Chromium` class is **o4-order-invariance ×2** (E15, ruled), not w7-destructive-seats. w7 ×6 fail on `toHaveCount` and timeouts (E17, ruled), plus `w7:133` new on CI.

### 2026-10-19 · repair r2 · `deploy-age` (run 39251853153 @ b675df143, job 118057945503)

**Measured.**
- `gh run view --job 118057945503 --log | grep -E "newest|FAIL"` → `newest successful deploy : 2026-07-07T02:19:20Z [gh run list --workflow deploy-pages.yml --status success]` · `newest recorded close : 2026-10-19T08:01:12-04:00 [git log -- docs/tranches (subject names a close)]` · `FAIL — production is older than the newest close by 104 day(s).` No override was set.
- `gh run list --workflow deploy-pages.yml --status success --limit 1 --json createdAt,headSha,event` → `2026-07-07T02:19:20Z`, `workflow_dispatch`, `80c58885…`. The five newest deploy-pages runs, from `2026-10-19T12:01:35Z` to `14:42:01Z`, are all `skipped` on `04d2d8089` (`workflow_run`).
- `git log -1 --date=iso-strict --format='%h %cd %s' -E --grep='(close|CLOSE|CLOSED|FINAL)' b675df143 -- docs/tranches` → `d706b5006 2026-10-19T08:01:12-04:00 docs(X·W11 §0gc · deploy-age r1): receipt — …`. The repo reproduces the CI reading exactly.

**The instrument's input defect, measured further than r1.** `git log --grep` searches the **whole message**, not the subject. The comment in the step says "subject names a close", and that is not what the query does.
- `git log -1 … --grep=… origin/master -- docs/tranches` → `80fe6c75b 2026-09-18T14:36:11-04:00 docs(x-v/w1.e): ESC-W1R-1 — ADOPT … evidence artefacts`. Its subject has no close token. The match is body line 31: `…returned to the wave's close seat with`.
- `git log --format=%s -E --grep=… 04d2d8089..b675df143 -- docs/tranches | wc -l` → **526** matching commits since the master tip. 447 of them have the token in the subject. 126 subjects are receipts, critics, reviews or `rN` passes, not closes.

**Does any honest close grammar change the verdict? No.** Each narrower reading is still past the deploy:
- Wave closes only (`… CLOSED` in a COHESION subject): `24f12aa24 2026-10-18T19:40:40-04:00 … X-W10 CLOSED`. That is 103 d past the deploy.
- Merged landing close on master: `2026-09-18T14:36:11-04:00` (r1 witness 35381701436). That is 73 d past.
- Strictest reading, the close **tags**: `git log -1 --format=%cd tranche-t-close` → `2026-07-13T02:56:56-04:00`, which is **6 d past** the 2026-07-07 deploy. (`tranche-s-close` is 2026-07-06 and `tranche-r-close` is 2026-07-04, both earlier than the deploy.)

So production has been older than a recorded close since tranche T closed. The DR-11 red is **true under every grammar**. Narrowing the grep is an input-precision repair, not a cure. Landing it alone would still leave the job red. It would also change what G-21 counts as a close, and that definition belongs to the same ruling as the topology. For both reasons it is folded into the escalation and not landed.

**Root cause (unchanged from r1, re-confirmed).** The gate self-locks:
1. `deploy-pages.yml` ships only on a `CI` `success` from a master push.
2. `deploy-age` is a job inside that `CI`.
3. Production is already older than the newest close.

So the only path to green is a deploy, and the only deploy that does not need this job green first is `workflow_dispatch`, which is forbidden. Every other red job could be cured and this one would still block the master push.

**Verdict.** NOT CURED in r2. Nothing was changed except this receipt: no ci.yml edit, override variable, threshold, `workflow_dispatch` or `if:` guard. ESC-W11-DEPLOY-AGE-SELF-LOCK stands, and its ruling scope is now wider:
- (A) move the check post-deploy, or (B) compare against the close reachable from the previously deployed sha (both from r1);
- **plus** a defined close grammar (subject-only `--format=%s` filtering, or the `tranche-*-close` tags) that replaces the whole-message grep.

   - So r1's "passed in the Linux container" ran arm64 Linux. CI's `ubuntu-latest` is x86_64, and SwiftShader's JIT backend differs by architecture. **r1 did not reproduce the CI platform, so "not reproduced off CI" does not stand as a negative finding.**
   - Every completed run fails this spec the same way:

     | run | job | result |
     |---|---|---|
     | 39034378132 | 117401250649 | red |
     | 39117135689 | 117645410730 | red |
     | 39204638324 | — | red |
     | 39229786770 | — | red |
     | 39251853153 | — | red |

     No CI run has ever passed `w12-ground` at any `--retries`.
   - The hang site varies. It is `w12-ground.spec.ts:81` (the `cold+1s` sample) on some attempts and `:82` (`cold+3s`) on others: in 117401250649 attempt 0 at `:81` and retries at `:82`; in 117645410730 every `#/` attempt at `:81`. The first clip screenshot therefore sometimes **succeeds** and the next one never returns. The compositor stops delivering frames 1–3 s after load.
   - The main thread stays alive. Each attempt's `error-context.md` (e.g. report `data/c588dd48….md`) carries a full ARIA page snapshot taken after the timeout. This is the Chromium sibling of the Linux-WebKit wedge recorded in §0gc e2e-safari (main thread alive, frame production stalled once the WebGL surfaces start), with the stall here on the frame side.
   - WebGPU is excluded as the cause. glass-ui's `useWebGPUCanvas` (`dist/color.wgsl-BTszLY39.js`, `l(e)`) rejects `isFallbackAdapter` and any swiftshader / llvmpipe / software adapter and falls back to WebGL2. Under CI's SwiftShader the atmosphere therefore runs on WebGL2.
   - The x86_64 reproduction was attempted. I exported HEAD to the scratchpad and pulled `--platform linux/amd64 mcr.microsoft.com/playwright:v1.60.0-noble`; ⟨`docker run --platform linux/amd64 busybox uname -m`⟩ → `x86_64`. I wrote the probe `probe.mjs`: one browser, one context closed in `finally`, DPR 1 vs DPR 2 arms, a 20 s per-shot timeout, and rAF and canvas-backing readouts. **The probe was refused at admission by the machine law.** ⟨`tail -1 ~/.claude/guard/log/zone.log`⟩ → `inuse=13208181`, and `guarded` line 168 refuses heavy runs at `zone_inuse() >= 13_000_000` ("reboot the Mac"). The run never started, so `guarded.log` has no `start` line for it. I killed the waiting `guarded` (pid 34749), confirmed no container named `w12-prep`, and deleted the export. Boot time is `Sat Oct 17 12:08:50 2026`.
   - Nothing is cured. Raising the 150 s test timeout, dropping DPR 2, or wrapping the screenshot in a retry would each mask the stall, which is forbidden.

### e2e-safari repair r2 — 2026-10-19 (seat: claude-opus-5-5; job `e2e-safari`, run 39251853153 @ `b675df143`, job 118057945192)

**Opened at** HEAD `28ce091e6` (tranche-u). `git status --porcelain` showed only other seats' rows (CARRY-LEDGER, X-W7L/W4 PNGs, W12U/W5 evidence, RENDERER.json, `scripts/dev/dev.sh`). None of them was touched. The only change this seat makes is this receipt.

**Commands and measured output.**
1. ⟨`gh run view -R mkbabb/value.js --job 118057945192 --log`⟩ gave 6,455 lines: `Running 178 tests using 1 worker` (12:30:49Z), then `80 failed · 7 flaky · 91 passed (2.2h)`. ⟨`gh run download 39251853153 -n e2e-safari-report`⟩ gave 133 error-context `.md` files and 6 PNGs.
2. **Byte delta against r1.** ⟨`git diff --stat 113b00bb1 b675df143 -- demo src e2e playwright.config.ts vite.config.ts .github package.json`⟩ produced **empty** output. The whole-tree diff is 1 file (+173), which is this receipt file. r1 (`113b00bb1`) and r2 (`b675df143`) therefore ran byte-identical product, spec, config and workflow.
3. **Set delta.** I compared the sorted unique failed+flaky titles of r1 (job 117985252506) and r2: 83 vs 87.
   - Only in r2: `w7-inspector-rows` ×7 (:77 :86 :108 :114 :120 :126 :137), `o18:638 (light)`, and `o1b-ground-luma-leap:37`.
   - Only in r1: `o12:122`, `o14:198`, `o22:76`, `o7:200`, and `w7-destructive-seats:133`.
   - Byte-identical inputs gave a different failing set. The outcome is therefore a function of the runner, not of the tree.
4. **Signature intensity.** `WebGL: context lost.` appears in r1.log 18 times and in r2.log **129** times. The messages cluster in o12/o15/o17 (log lines 1000–1500), 3000–4400, and the smoke-safari WebGL specs (dual-webgl ×2, mix-flow, sustained-30s). There is 1 `PaneChunkError: pane chunk not settled after 20000 ms`.
5. **The new w7-inspector-rows cells.** No WebGL assertion is involved. Attempt 1 fails at `verdict(page, "Rename failed: Server exploded")` (8 s). Retries 1 and 2 fail one step earlier, at `wallFailing`'s `getByRole('article', { name: 'Palette: Owned One' })` (8 s). The error-context snapshot shows the dock booted (`Browse` selected, `@mbabb` signed in) but `region "Browse"` and `region "Palettes"` are **both empty**: the routed pane never mounted inside 8 s. In r1 this spec passed on the same bytes. This is the stalled-web-process class recorded in r1's root cause (step 5 of r1: the pane-chunk/test deadlines are missed while the WebGL path wedges the WebKit process), now reaching a non-WebGL spec that runs later in the single-worker queue.
6. **Local real-GPU repro: REFUSED by the zone gate.** ⟨`rm -rf node_modules/.vite-e2e-9571; VJS_E2E_PORT=9571 VJS_E2E_PERF_PORT=9572 ~/.claude/guard/guarded --exclusive webkit --timeout 40 -- npx playwright test --project=oracles-safari --workers=1 --retries=0 --reporter=line e2e/smoke/oracles/w7-inspector-rows.spec.ts e2e/smoke/oracles/cold-nav-scene-enter.spec.ts e2e/smoke/oracles/o1b-ground-luma-leap.spec.ts e2e/smoke/oracles/w7-destructive-seats.spec.ts`⟩ printed `[guarded] waiting for headroom (kernel zone data.kalloc.1024 at 13.2M (panics near 20.8M): reboot the Mac)` and never started. `~/.claude/guard/zone-kalloc1024` reads `13220265`. I stopped the waiting guard (pid 32599). No browser, server or vite process was started. Per machine law, heavy work stops here and the owner is told to reboot. The r1 darwin witness stands: the same spec families pass on a real Apple GPU, 32/39 with only ruled rows red, and cold-nav + o21-rail 12/12.

**Root cause (unchanged from r1).** The hosted `macos-15` runner is an Apple-Virtualization VM with no real GPU acceleration. The WebKit WebGL path loses contexts and stalls the web process, so the app's and the specs' deadlines are missed. How hard it degrades varies from run to run (context losses ×18 → ×129 on identical bytes), which is why the failing set moves (72 → 80) with no code change.

**What would mask it, and so was not done.** Withholding WebGL or forcing DPR 1 in the WebKit projects, raising the 8 s / 20 s / 30 s deadlines, reordering specs to escape the stall, skipping or excluding the spec families, adding retries, or `continue-on-error`.

**Disposition.** Not cured in-seat. No files changed except this receipt.
- **ESC-W11-SAFARI-HOSTED-GPU** stands, re-witnessed with a byte-identical A/B: e2e-safari needs a runner with real GPU-accelerated WebKit (self-hosted Apple silicon, or a hosted size whose GPU is verified). This is an owner billing or infrastructure act.
- **ESC-W11-ZONE-GATE-REBOOT** (co-signed): the local real-GPU re-witness of the r2-only cells is blocked until the Mac is rebooted (zone 13.22M ≥ 13M).
- ESC-W11-SAFARI-RULED-ROWS (25 darwin cells) and ESC-W11-ADMIN-PANE-DEV-FRAGMENT (w7-destructive-seats navigates Admin views; owner X-DS, `demo/**`) stand unchanged.

### X.W11.ci1b (§0gd orchestrator unit; `.ci1` receipt act 6) — DONE: `ci.yml` push filter re-includes the five docs fixtures by `!` negation; commit `d09d0b06b`

**Seat**: claude-opus-5-5 · 2026-10-19. W11.md ADDENDA fourth–sixth read; COHESION §0gc.3 (`:4319-4323`), §0gd (`:4353`), §0ge (`:4403`, `.s1` = e2e-safari → `webkit-hosted.yml`) located by grep; this record's `.ci1` receipt (`:2182`) read whole; nothing else.

**Crash-recovery.** ⟨`git status --porcelain -- .github/workflows/ci.yml docs/tranches/X/execution/A/X-W11.md`⟩ → ∅. Nothing inherited. `.s1` had not landed (⟨`ls .github/workflows/`⟩ → no `webkit-hosted.yml`; ⟨`git log -1 -- .github/workflows/ci.yml`⟩ → `40fad4305`), so the not-concurrent-with-`.s1` lock holds.

**Acts, in order.**
1. **Anchor measured.** ⟨`sed -n 1,25p .github/workflows/ci.yml`⟩ → `:15 paths-ignore:` / `:16 - "docs/**"`, with no re-include. It matches the baseline at `:2325`.
2. **Five fixtures re-measured at their readers.** a16 receipt: `e2e/smoke/a11y-select-title.spec.ts:499-501`. W6 ask + census addendum: `e2e/smoke/views/gradient.spec.ts:724,730`. o25 frames: `e2e/smoke/oracles/o25-atmosphere-response.spec.ts:39,138,146-147` reads `atmo1-{ref,h030,h120,h210,h300}.png`, re-included as `atmo1-*.png`; the sibling `capture-atmo1.mjs` and `i2-*.txt` are not read and stay excluded. MANIFEST: `e2e/visual/golden-integrity.spec.ts:48`. ⟨`git ls-files --error-unmatch` ×4 + `git ls-files …/atmosphere`⟩ → all tracked.
3. **Semantics.** GitHub honours `!` only in `paths`, and `paths` and `paths-ignore` cannot both be set on one event. So the filter becomes `paths: ["**", "!docs/**", <5 re-includes>]`. A path's last matching pattern decides it, so every re-include comes after the exclude. `pull_request` stays unfiltered (`null`), and `cancel-in-progress: true` stays.
4. **Last-match-wins simulation.** ⟨node, minimatch `{dot:true}` over the parsed `on.push.paths`⟩ → `RUN src/index.ts` · `RUN .github/workflows/ci.yml` · `skip docs/…/X-W11.md` · `RUN` for each of the five fixtures (atmo1-ref + h030 sampled) · `skip …/atmosphere/capture-atmo1.mjs`.
5. **Commit `d09d0b06b`**, `ci(X·W11.ci1b §0gd): ci.yml push filter re-includes the five docs fixtures …`. It changes 1 path (`ci.yml`, +16/−6).

**Gates (BEFORE → AFTER).**

|---|---|---|---|
| actionlint ×2 | ⟨`actionlint .github/workflows/ci.yml`⟩ ×2 | rc 0 (`.ci1`) | rc 0 ×2 — **GREEN** |
| YAML parse ×2 | ⟨node `yaml.parse` → `on`, `concurrency`⟩ ×2 | `{"branches":["**"],"paths-ignore":["docs/**"]}` | `{"branches":["**"],"paths":["**","!docs/**",…5]}` · `pull_request: null` · `cancel-in-progress: true`, rc 0 ×2 — **GREEN** |
| five fixtures re-included (grep) | ⟨`grep -c '^            - "docs/' .github/workflows/ci.yml`⟩ | `0` | `5` — **GREEN** |

### X.W11.ci4 (§0gc.6, RESUME sixth) — ESCALATED: the 390 overflow re-measured RED ×2; cause re-confirmed PRODUCER at the installed glass 10.1.0 bytes; no consumer cure exists that is not a mask (ESC-W11ci4-1)

Seat: claude-opus-5-5 · 2026-10-19 · HEAD `a5f915af6`. Writable set `demo/shell/dock/**` + this record. No product byte was written.

1. **Crash-recovery.** ⟨`git status --porcelain -- demo/shell/dock docs/tranches/X/execution/A/X-W11.md`⟩ → ∅. Nothing inherited.
2. **Re-measure (guarded browser run, one browser, one context per run, closed in `finally`).** I used a scratch probe (`ci4-probe.mjs`) that serves `npx vite --port 8391 --strictPort`. It loads `/` at 1440×900, lets the desktop dock (`collapse="open"`) collapse after a body click, then samples the document every rAF for 2.5 s across `setViewportSize(390×844)` (`isDesktop` → false → `collapse=false` = always-expanded, so the dock expands in the same tick as its composition narrows). ⟨`~/.claude/guard/guarded --timeout 12 -- node ci4-probe.mjs 2`⟩ →
   - run 0: pre `collapsed`, `--dock-expanded-px 487px`; **max overflow 113 px** (`scrollWidth 503 / clientWidth 390`, dock `offsetWidth 487`, `data-morphing` = 1) over 57 frames, t = 24 to 561 ms; rest `390/390`, dock `188`, `--dock-expanded-px 188px`.
   - run 1: the same; **113 px**, 51 frames, t = 40 to 578 ms; rest `390/390`, `188px`.
   - Control: with the dock never collapsed, the same resize reads overflow 0 ×2. The defect is only on a collapsed→expanded flip that crosses the content change.
   - **Gate "390 full-layer morph: scrollWidth == clientWidth ×2": RED ×2** (BEFORE = the O-101 reading 524/390, NOW = 503/390; the spread is the 1440 composition's width, 508 → 487 px).
3. **Cause, at the installed bytes.** `node_modules/@mkbabb/glass-ui` `"version": "10.1.0"`, `dist/dock.js:456` reads `e === "layout" && (o.value ? c = m : l = m)`. The expanded endpoint `c` moves only on a layout capture taken while expanded. The collapsed full pane narrows (487 → 188 resting) and the next expand reserves the stale `inline-size: var(--dock-expanded-px)` (`layers.css:116-118`). The visible scale also rides that endpoint, so the 0.55 s frame is a wrong-size dock, not only a wide box. This is O-101's R-1 exactly (`ff7ebe113`; BK mirror `../glass-ui/docs/tranches/BK/coordination/valuejs-outbound-2026-10-19-dock-stale-expanded-endpoint.md` present).
4. **Why no consumer cure is lawful** (each arm was weighed against the bytes):
   - (a) `max-inline-size` on the dock or its host. The host is a flex item of the centred `.dock-band`, so a percentage cap on GlassDock resolves against a content-sized host and does nothing. A working cap needs `100vw`, or a host `min/max-inline-size` pair. Either one fires only while the defect is live, so it is a fallback that hides it. It also squeezes a 487 px wrong-target dock into the band instead of morphing to the right size. Glass deleted exactly this consumer cap as harmful (`glass shell.css:199`, BK #47 W3), and O-101 asks "no consumer clip, copy or override". Refused: a HIGH masking defect under the standing law.
   - (b) `overflow-x: clip` on the host or band. A clip. Refused by the LOCKS.
   - (c) `:key="isDesktop"` on `<GlassDock>`, which remounts at the breakpoint. It contradicts `Dock.vue:50` ("Dock is the seat at every width — desktop and mobile are one seat"). It discards producer state to dodge a stale cache. Refused as a workaround.

| run | seed 30 | seed 150 | seed 260 | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | FP 184ms · ΔE_OK `0.1595` | FP 60ms · `0.2933` | FP 112ms · `0.2433` | `1 failed`, exit 1 |
| 2 | FP 2184ms · `0.1595` | FP 60ms · `0.2933` | FP 72ms · `0.2433` | `1 failed`, exit 1 |

   Every seed paints the light first-visit pair `[#b37290 #df8ea7 #ffb0b4 #ffcfc8]` at first paint. 3. **Mechanism (a) measured: a build-time seeded-ground table keyed by hue band cannot meet the 0.02 band.** The settled ground is not a function of hue. ⟨`node scratchpad/derive-spread.mjs`⟩ (glass-ui's own `deriveAurora` from the installed `dist/aurora.js`, seeds across L 0.45–0.8 × C 0.08–0.26 × h ±5° of each oracle hue, max per-stop ΔE_OK against the `oklch(0.62 0.2 h)` derive) → `hue 30: 0.211` · `hue 150: 0.201` · `hue 260: 0.195`. Any one hue-band entry is ~10× the tolerance off for most seeds that share its band. A table that also keys on L and C is a second derive, interpolated in the inline script, which §4.3 forbids. The settled ground is also not the raw derive. Seed 150 raw `[#4e6100 #238f00 #00b273 #00d5b7]` against settled `[#5f721d #3ba124 #31c585 #38e9cb]`: in light scheme the ground is `resolvedPalette` (`useAtmosphere.ts:208`), the calibrated field through `resolveCalibratedAtmosphere` (`atmosphere-calibration.ts`) and `resolveAtoms`. `ground.ts` holds none of that chain, so no `ground.ts`-sourced table can carry it.
4. **Mechanism (b) measured: holding first paint until hydration has no primitive reachable from these files.** The seeded ground's first write is the sink's immediate watch at `app.mount` (`main.ts:102-103`, after `await router.isReady()`). The browser's first paint lands at 60–184 ms, long before that. An inline head script cannot hold the browser's paint across an async module mount. `<link rel=expect blocking=render>` unblocks at parse end. `blocking=render` on a script unblocks at fetch or execute, not at a top-level-await mount, and WebKit does not ship it (the `oracles-safari` cell collects this spec). Leaving the ground unwritten for a seeded load makes the oracle's `atPaint` null, which fails at `:131`. Hiding the root paints the UA canvas instead: a white first frame on a dark scheme, the F-6 breach `ground.ts` exists to prevent. That is a mask, not a cure. Either way, FCP/LCP on every share-link load moves against `.p2`'s LCP ≤ 2500 budget.
5. **The cure that would turn the gate lies outside the bounds.** A parser-blocking classic `<script src>` in `<head>` is render-blocking in every engine. To use it, the calibrated seeded-ground resolver (URL seed → `parsePickerColor` → `resolveCalibratedAtmosphere`/`resolveAtoms` → `normalizeGroundStops`) must be built as its own pre-module bundle. `ground.ts` would export it as the single source, and `hydrate.ts` would read its result from a boot global instead of re-parsing the hash. That needs a new vite build input (`vite.config.ts`), the token plugin (`plugins/vite-ground-tokens.ts`), and a resolver module that pulls `atmosphere-calibration.ts` and glass-ui's derive into a render-blocking head script. All of these are outside the writable set, and none is a minimal adjacent edit (a different concern: the build graph and the critical path). It also adds a synchronous head script to every load, which collides with `.p2`'s critical-path cuts. This is a design decision for the orchestrator and `.p2` together, not for this seat.

**Gate.** O28-114: RED ×2 → **RED** (unchanged; no product bytes landed).

**Escalation ESC-W11h7-1.** Specified cure impossible at the bytes:
- (a) a hue-band table is measured at ΔE_OK 0.195–0.211 spread within a ±5° band, against a 0.02 tolerance, and cannot carry the calibrated field chain.
- (b) no engine primitive holds first paint from these three files across the async mount, and the blank-hold variant fails the oracle and breaches F-6.

Grant sought:
- the pre-module seeded-ground bundle in item 5, with `vite.config.ts`, `plugins/vite-ground-tokens.ts` and a new `demo/app/composables/boot/seeded-ground.ts`, co-planned with `.p2`, because it adds to the critical path;
- or a ruling that O28-114's first-paint arm is held to `source: "storage" | "default"` loads, with the URL arm BLOCKED-ON the bundle.

**Residuals.** `.p2`'s ordering note "E7 index.html before `.p2`" is released: `.h7` landed no bytes in `index.html`.

**Adjacent edits.** None. **Commits.** This receipt only.

4. **Cure** (`formatSpecimen`, after the V8C-01 branch, before the CSS branch). `PARSE_CLAMPED_SPACES = {hsl, hwb}`. `leavesParseClamp` tests every non-hue channel against its `PICKER_CHANNELS` [min, max], **on the budgeted digits the CSS row would print**, so float noise at a bound (white's `l = 1.0000000000002`, cf. ESC-W11b-S3-WHITE-HSL) never flips the form. A hit returns `` `${space} · h · s×100 · l×100${alpha}` `` with `form: "channels"`. Each channel is budgeted **after** the ×100 (V8C-01's serialized-domain rule, which absorbs the `-16537.899999999998` class), and `outOfGamut` is unchanged. No projection and no `src/**` edit.
